- **pstack-perf-issue**: Pstack perf role: trace measured slowness against a baseline ([marcelormendes/poteto-pi](https://github.com/marcelormendes/poteto-pi/blob/HEAD/agents/pstack-perf-issue.md))
- **pstack-poteto-agent**: Pstack delegate for a task owned by a poteto-mode coordinator. Reads the bundled pstack mode and works within the assigned scope. ([dmae97/omk](https://github.com/dmae97/omk/blob/HEAD/packages/coding-agent/resources/pstack/agents/pstack-poteto-agent.md) · ★153)
- **pstack-poteto-agent**: Bounded implementation in Poteto style ([punk-dev-robot/dotfiles](https://github.com/punk-dev-robot/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/config/custom/pi/agent/agents/pstack-poteto-agent.md) · ★1)
- **pstack-poteto-agent**: Delegate subagent for code-writing and helper tasks spawned during a `/pstack-poteto-mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/pstack-poteto-mode` itself. Spawn a fresh `pstack-poteto-agent` for each new task, and resume one only in the strict cases that pstack-poteto-mode's Subagents section names. Reads the `pstack-poteto-mode` skill's `SKILL.md` in full before any work, including its inline Principles index. Substituting `generalPurpose` skips that read and drifts. ([sergical/pstack-skills](https://github.com/sergical/pstack-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/pstack-harness/agents/pstack-poteto-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **pstack-reader**: Read-only delegate for pstack's explorers, investigators and reviewers. Spawn it where a pstack skill names `pstack-reader`. It has no file-editing tools, and the pstack hook denies its shell commands that write. ([EvanBatten/pstack-for-cc](https://github.com/EvanBatten/pstack-for-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/pstack-reader.md) · ★2)
- **pstack-reader**: Read-only pstack worker for exploring, explaining, reviewing, and judging. Keeps MCP, web, and skills; has no edit or write tools. Use where a pstack skill asks for a readonly or read-only subagent. ([proguha/pstack-pi](https://github.com/proguha/pstack-pi/blob/HEAD/agents/pstack-reader.md) · 2 copies)
- **pstack-readonly**: Read-only pstack worker for explorers, investigators, reviewers, and judges. Can read files and run inspection commands, cannot edit. Use wherever a pstack skill asks for readonly true. ([charlietlamb/cstack](https://github.com/charlietlamb/cstack/blob/HEAD/pstack/agents/pstack-readonly.md) · ★1)
- **pstack-readonly**: Read-only delegate for pstack skills that ask for Cursor's generalPurpose subagent with readonly true. Reads and searches, never edits. ([hoesler/agent-stuff](https://github.com/hoesler/agent-stuff/blob/HEAD/pi/extensions/pstack/pstack/docs/harness/agents/pstack-readonly.md))
- **pstack-readonly**: Read-only pstack worker for review panels, explorers, and explainers that skills such as `how` and `arena` spawn. Reads code and runs read-only commands. Never edits files. Has no MCP tools, so skills that need MCP evidence spawn `general-purpose` instead. ([RayenTellissy/pstack-claude](https://github.com/RayenTellissy/pstack-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/pstack-readonly.md))
- **pstack-readonly**: Read-only explorer, investigator, reviewer, or judge for pstack skills. Reads code, searches, runs non-mutating commands, and reports findings. Never edits files. ([slappcard/pi-pstack-skills](https://github.com/slappcard/pi-pstack-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/pstack-readonly.md))

- **ptr-channel**: Use to run one coding package ("channel") for the PTR head in a dedicated git worktree. Trigger when the head hands over a package with a goal, a list of owned files and a base branch. Edits only the owned files, never commits, pushes, merges, tags or freezes, and hands back once with changed files, real command outputs and open points. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-channel.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-claims-auditor**: Use to audit PTR docs, READMEs, STATUS.md, component.toml text, PR descriptions or experiment result write-ups for over-claiming. Trigger before publishing or merging any text that says a component or model "improves", "outperforms", "specialises", "is implemented", "passes" or cites numbers, and when STATUS.md, docs/components/STATUS.md or research/novelty/ change. Read-only; reports verdicts and replacement wording, never edits. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-claims-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-experiment-designer**: Use to draft a falsifiable experiment design for a PTR research question, such as "do typed slots help hard-constraint retention" or "does verifier-precedence weak supervision calibrate better than majority vote". Trigger when someone proposes a new experiment, a successor to a prepared or inconclusive one, or asks how to test a claim from research/novelty/CLAIMS.md. Produces the design and the exact scripts/new_experiment.py command in its reply only; read-only, never creates the experiment. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-experiment-designer.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-fact-checker**: Use to fact-check changed PTR docs, STATUS files and write-ups against the recorded evidence. Trigger before a head merges documentation or a result write-up that cites numbers, verdicts, hashes, tags, seed lists, mutation counts or file:line locations. Read-only; reports a table of mismatches with severity and never edits. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-fact-checker.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-invariant-reviewer**: Use to review a diff, branch or PR in the PTR repository against the 17 binding invariants in docs/INVARIANTS.md, docs/COMPONENT_CONTRACTS.md and the CLAUDE.md conventions. Trigger before merging or opening a PR that touches crates/, bins/, proto/ or model/, especially changes to ptr-runtime, ptr-ledger, ptr-state, ptr-pg, ptr-search, ptr-security, ptr-exec, ptr-verifier, ptr-branch, ptr-fastmem or ptr-lineage, or when asked "does this violate an invariant?". Read-only; reports findings, never fixes them. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-invariant-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ptr-rereviewer**: Use for an adversarial, read-only re-review of one channel round in the PTR repository. Trigger after a channel hands back and before the head integrates, or when the head asks "is this round clean?". Reads the diff against the base, re-runs read-only and Python checks, and reports findings P0/P1/P2 with file:line and a verdict. Never edits. ([xrey167/PTR](https://github.com/xrey167/PTR/blob/HEAD/.claude/plugins/ptr-dev/agents/ptr-rereviewer.md) · ★1)
- **ptu-availability**: Looks up Azure PTU and PAYG Standard model availability by running the installed `ptu availability` command. - **ptu-availability-direct**: Independently checks Azure PTU and PAYG Standard availability without running the installed `ptu` tool. - **pty-expert**: Expert on the Unix process lifecycle on macOS — forkpty, exec, process groups, signals, waitpid, descriptors. Su única función es impedir que por descuido se divulgue información - secretos, claves, datos personales, datos de negocios o clientes reales, rutas y cuentas del equipo, capturas con datos, historial de git - y dar un veredicto de PUBLICABLE o BLOQUEADO. - **publish-guard**: Guardián de publicación, exclusivo para que nada sensible llegue al repositorio público de GitHub. Usar SIEMPRE antes de cada `git push` (de cualquier rama o etiqueta) y antes de unir una rama a `main`. Revisa lo que se va a subir — archivos y cada commit nuevo — en busca de contraseñas, claves, tokens, `.env`, datos de personas reales, rutas y correos personales, volcados de base de datos, capturas con información privada y archivos que no deberían estar en git. - **pull-request**: Confirmation-gated PR creation agent. Reads recent commits on the current branch, drafts a title (&lt;=70 chars) and body (Summary + Test plan), shows the draft to the user, and waits for an explicit "yes" before pushing the branch and running gh pr create. Spawns conflict-resolver on any merge conflict detected before the push, then retries once after resolution. Use when: user types /pull-request; is in the commit-&gt;push-&gt;PR flow via /commit-push-pr; or asks to "open a PR", "create a pull request", or "push and open a PR for this branch". Has write access — its diff is final for this PVD loop (re-verifying it would recurse indefinitely). - **pvd-verifier**: Read-only verifier in the Prover–Verifier Deliberation protocol. Invoked by the prover (main Claude Code session) after each code change. Capability-asymmetric on purpose: this subagent is Haiku, the prover is Sonnet — the asymmetry is the source of the selection signal per the paper. ([jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation](https://github.com/jsedoc/prover-verifier-deliberation/blob/HEAD/skills/prover-verifier-code/agents/pvd-verifier.md) · ★3)

- **pw-writer-task**: Turn per-task decisions into task docs — precise ([zulffaza/agentic-project-workflow](https://github.com/zulffaza/agentic-project-workflow/blob/HEAD/tooling/agents/pw-writer-task.md))

- **pwa-architect**: Designs progressive web app features for games. Use when adding offline support, install prompts, or PWA capabilities. ([sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins](https://github.com/sponticelli/gamedev-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-games/agents/pwa-architect.md) · ★19)
- **pwa-architect**: Progressive Web App specialist — service workers, Workbox caching strategies, Web App Manifest, offline-first design, installability, and platform quirks (especially iOS Safari limits). Use proactively for PWA architecture, service worker lifecycle, offline support, storage strategy, and converting web/React apps to installable PWAs. ([lakarpusky/claude-epc-workflow](https://github.com/lakarpusky/claude-epc-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/pwa-architect.md))
- **pwa-architect**: Use at the start of any non-trivial feature, a new matter (sprawa), a library choice, or a change that spans core + content + UI + assistant. Plans user flow, files, data, owner agents, risks and acceptance criteria for the PWA. Read-only; returns a plan. - **pwa-auditor**: Use when auditing a web URL for PWA readiness — manifest completeness, service worker behavior, offline fallback, install prompt UX, icon coverage, apple-touch-icon. - **pwa-build-verify**: Run tsc, eslint, and next build in this PWA workspace and report pass/fail with concise error summaries. Use after any code change to verify the workspace is healthy. - **pwa-checklist**: The unit tests and `e2e/installed-app.spec.ts` cover what a desktop Chromium can emulate. ([NosFabrica/Brainstorm-UI](https://github.com/NosFabrica/Brainstorm-UI/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/pwa-checklist.md) · ★6)
- **pwa-debug**: Use this agent to debug PWA issues in Suiviseries — service worker registration/caching (custom-sw.js, ngsw-config.json), app-update prompts (UpdateService / SwUpdate), and Web Push subscription/permission problems (PushNotificationService). Invoke for "update not detected", stale cache, push not arriving, or SW-only-in-prod confusion. - **pwa-engineer**: Builds the PWA platform in apps/pwa: app shell, router, layout, Vite and vite-plugin-pwa config, manifest, service worker and update prompt, install flow (incl. iOS), offline, IndexedDB storage, settings, i18n runtime, persona deep links, phone frame on desktop, Vercel config. - **pwauto-app-explorer**: Explores a web application with the Playwright CLI browser and the site crawler and records what it learns (purpose, actors, screens, journeys, rules, terms, gaps) as a sourced domainlore knowledge base in knowledge/. Use before writing scenarios for a new application or area, or when the application changed. Not for writing scenarios or test code. ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-app-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **pwauto-env-setup**: Analyses the user's computer (Windows, macOS, Linux) and installs and verifies everything pwauto needs — Node.js check, npm packages, Playwright browsers, .env, agent skills, plugins. Use on a fresh clone, when tests cannot start, or when the user asks to set up or install. Not for exploring the application or writing tests. ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-env-setup.md) · ★1)
- **pwauto-framework-architect**: Owns the pwauto framework core (src/core, src/steps/common, playwright.config.ts, CI, lint rules) and extends it without breaking its architecture — configuration, fixtures, utilities, authentication, reporters, browsers, environments, pipelines. Use for any change to the framework itself rather than to one application's tests. Not for one application's page objects, steps or scenarios (use pwauto-test-automator). ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-framework-architect.md) · ★1)
- **pwauto-report-analyst**: Runs the pwauto suite or a subset and turns the results into a plain-language run summary (pass rate, failures by cause, flaky tests, known application bugs, coverage gaps, slowest scenarios) with links to screenshots, videos, traces and logs. Use when the user asks to run tests, for results, or for a report for the team. Not for fixing failures (use pwauto-test-healer). ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-report-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **pwauto-scenario-designer**: Designs risk-based BDD scenarios in Gherkin from the knowledge base (happy, negative, boundary, role and state cases), writes them as @draft feature files with a test plan and a traceability table. Use after exploration, or when the user asks for test cases or scenarios. Not for step definitions or page objects (use pwauto-test-automator). ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-scenario-designer.md) · ★1)
- **pwauto-test-automator**: Automates approved BDD scenarios with the pwauto framework — page objects, step definitions, API data setup — then runs them until they pass for the right reason and are stable. Use when scenarios are approved (no @draft) and need step definitions, or when the user asks to automate or implement tests. Not for designing scenarios, or for diagnosing failures of existing tests (use pwauto-test-healer). ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-test-automator.md) · ★1)
- **pwauto-test-healer**: Diagnoses failing or flaky pwauto tests from the report evidence, classifies each failure (locator, timing, test data, flaky environment, application bug, wrong expectation, setup), fixes test bugs without weakening tests and records application bugs as Gaps and bug reports. Use when tests fail or are flaky. Not for new scenarios or new automation (use pwauto-test-automator). ([quark-sri/pwauto](https://github.com/quark-sri/pwauto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pwauto-test-healer.md) · ★1)
- **pwc-scout**: Specialized agent for finding trending papers with linked code on Papers with Code. Use when the main oss-veda skill needs research-side signals or the user asks about recent AI papers with implementations. ([anujdevsingh/oss-veda](https://github.com/anujdevsingh/oss-veda/blob/HEAD/plugins/oss-veda/agents/pwc-scout.md) · ★2)
